- **analyze**: Cross-check `spec.md`, `plan.md`, `tasks.md` (and `clarify.md` if present) for drift between intent and implementation plan. Use AFTER all three pipeline artifacts exist and BEFORE implementation begins, OR mid-implementation when something feels off. Output is `specs/&lt;NNN&gt;-&lt;slug&gt;/analyze.md` — an advisory report of drift findings (critical / warning / info).
